As usual, the Grand Prix weekend started one day before the actual Grand Prix. On Friday, players were able to take part in a number of side events, all fun, some serious. Among the more serious were the last-chance Grand Prix Trials, offering people their final chance to earn two byes for the main event and offering us the first glimpse into what decks to expect over the course of the weekend ...
